Original source text:
Polar Ship Certificate validity, survey dates and endorsements shall be harmonized with the relevant SOLAS certificates in accordance with the provisions of SOLAS regulation I/14. The certificate shall include a supplement, recording equipment required by the Code.
Normalized requirement:
The Polar Ship Certificate validity dates, survey dates, and endorsements shall match the applicable SOLAS certificate schedule, and polar_certificate_supplement_present shall be true with required equipment records.
Required inputs: Polar and SOLAS certificate dates, endorsements, and equipment supplement

```turtle
        sh:message "Polar Ship Certificate validity, survey, and endorsement dates must match the corresponding categorized SOLAS certificate schedule dates." ;

            PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>
            SELECT $this
            WHERE {
                {

                              nltl:hasCertificateScheduleDateRecord ?record .
                        ?record nltl:certificateScheduleDate ?value ;
                                nltl:certificateScheduleDateType nltl:validityDateCategory .
                    }
                }
                UNION
                {

                                nltl:certificateScheduleDateType nltl:endorsementDateCategory .
                    }
                }

                        $this nltl:polarCertificateValidityDates ?value
                    }
                }
                UNION
```

```turtle
gen:IMO_017_ShipShape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass nltl:ship ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:polarCertificateValidityDates ;
        sh:datatype xsd:date ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:message "The ship shall provide Polar Ship Certificate validity dates."
    ] ;

        sh:datatype xsd:date ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:message "The ship shall provide survey dates."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:endorsementDates ;
        sh:datatype xsd:date ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:message "The ship shall provide endorsement dates."
    ] ;

        sh:path nltl:polarCertificateSupplementPresent ;
        sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:hasValue true ;
        sh:message "The Polar Ship Certificate supplement shall be present."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:hasPolarShipCertificate ;
        sh:class nltl:polarShipCertificateForm ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:message "The ship shall have a Polar Ship Certificate."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:hasPolarCertificateSupplement ;
        sh:class nltl:polarCertificateSupplement ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:message "The Polar Ship Certificate shall include a supplement."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:hasSolasCertificateSchedule ;
        sh:class nltl:solasCertificateSchedule ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:message "The ship shall have the applicable SOLAS certificate schedule."
    ] ;
    sh:sparql [
        a sh:SPARQLConstraint ;
        sh:message "Polar Ship Certificate validity, survey, and endorsement dates shall match the corresponding SOLAS certificate schedule dates." ;
        sh:select """
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            PREFIX sh: <http://www.w3.org/ns/shacl#>
            PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>

            SELECT $this
            WHERE {
                {
                    $this nltl:polarCertificateValidityDates ?date .
                    FILTER (datatype(?date) = xsd:date)
                    FILTER NOT EXISTS {
                        $this nltl:hasSolasCertificateSchedule ?schedule .
                        ?schedule nltl:hasCertificateScheduleDateRecord ?record .
                        ?record nltl:certificateScheduleDateType nltl:validityDateCategory ;
                                nltl:certificateScheduleDate ?scheduleDate .
                        FILTER (datatype(?scheduleDate) = xsd:date)
                        FILTER (?scheduleDate = ?date)
                    }
                }
                UNION
                {
                    $this nltl:surveyDates ?date .
                    FILTER (datatype(?date) = xsd:date)
                    FILTER NOT EXISTS {
                        $this nltl:hasSolasCertificateSchedule ?schedule .
                        ?schedule nltl:hasCertificateScheduleDateRecord ?record .
                        ?record nltl:certificateScheduleDateType nltl:surveyDateCategory ;
                                nltl:certificateScheduleDate ?scheduleDate .
                        FILTER (datatype(?scheduleDate) = xsd:date)
                        FILTER (?scheduleDate = ?date)
                    }
                }
                UNION
                {
                    $this nltl:endorsementDates ?date .
                    FILTER (datatype(?date) = xsd:date)
                    FILTER NOT EXISTS {
                        $this nltl:hasSolasCertificateSchedule ?schedule .
                        ?schedule nltl:hasCertificateScheduleDateRecord ?record .
                        ?record nltl:certificateScheduleDateType nltl:endorsementDateCategory ;
                                nltl:certificateScheduleDate ?scheduleDate .
                        FILTER (datatype(?scheduleDate) = xsd:date)
                        FILTER (?scheduleDate = ?date)
                    }
                }
            }
        """
    ] .
```

```turtle
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:message "The survey date is required and must be an xsd:date."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:endorsementDates ;
        sh:datatype xsd:date ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:message "The endorsement date is required and must be an xsd:date."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:polarCertificateSupplementPresent ;
        sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:hasValue true ;
        sh:message "The Polar Ship Certificate shall include a supplement."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:hasPolarCertificateSupplement ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:class nltl:polarCertificateSupplement ;
        sh:node <urn:nltl:generated-shape:IMO-017-SupplementShape> ;
        sh:message "A Polar Certificate supplement with required equipment records is required."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:hasPolarShipCertificate ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:class nltl:polarShipCertificateForm ;
        sh:message "The ship shall have a Polar Ship Certificate."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:hasSolasCertificateSchedule ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:class nltl:solasCertificateSchedule ;
        sh:node <urn:nltl:generated-shape:IMO-017-SolasScheduleShape> ;
        sh:message "The applicable SOLAS certificate schedule is required."
    ] ;
    sh:sparql [
        a sh:SPARQLConstraint ;
        sh:message "Each SOLAS certificate schedule date shall match the corresponding Polar Ship Certificate date category." ;
        sh:select """
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            PREFIX sh: <http://www.w3.org/ns/shacl#>
            PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>

            SELECT $this
            WHERE {
                $this nltl:hasSolasCertificateSchedule /
                      nltl:hasCertificateScheduleDateRecord ?record .
                ?record nltl:certificateScheduleDateType ?category ;
                        nltl:certificateScheduleDate ?scheduleDate .
                FILTER (
                    (
                        ?category = nltl:validityDateCategory &&
                        NOT EXISTS {
                            $this nltl:polarCertificateValidityDates ?scheduleDate
                        }
                    ) ||
                    (
                        ?category = nltl:surveyDateCategory &&
                        NOT EXISTS {
                            $this nltl:surveyDates ?scheduleDate
                        }
                    ) ||
                    (
                        ?category = nltl:endorsementDateCategory &&
                        NOT EXISTS {
                            $this nltl:endorsementDates ?scheduleDate
                        }
                    )
                )
            }
        """
    ] .

<urn:nltl:generated-shape:IMO-017-SolasScheduleShape> a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:hasCertificateScheduleDateRecord ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:class nltl:certificateScheduleDateRecord ;
        sh:node <urn:nltl:generated-shape:IMO-017-DateRecordShape> ;
        sh:message "The SOLAS certificate schedule shall contain certificate schedule date records."
    ] .

<urn:nltl:generated-shape:IMO-017-DateRecordShape> a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:certificateScheduleDate ;
        sh:datatype xsd:date ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:message "A certificate schedule date record shall contain an xsd:date."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:certificateScheduleDateType ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:in (
            nltl:validityDateCategory
            nltl:surveyDateCategory
            nltl:endorsementDateCategory
        ) ;
        sh:message "A certificate schedule date record shall have a permitted validity, survey, or endorsement category."
    ] .

<urn:nltl:generated-shape:IMO-017-SupplementShape> a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:hasRequiredEquipmentRecords ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:class nltl:requiredEquipmentRecords ;
        sh:message "The Polar Certificate supplement shall record the required equipment."
    ] .

```

A. STABLE IDENTITY AND SOURCE EVIDENCE
Audit order: 85
Batch: BATCH_09
Requirement ID: IMO-074
Source family: IMO
Verification mode: DIRECT_STATIC
Source document: International Code for Ships Operating in Polar Waters (Polar Code)
Source revision: Resolution MSC.385(94), adopted 21 Nov 2014; consolidated with MEPC.264(68), adopted 15 May 2015
Clause / section / page: 8.3.3.3.3.1 / Part I-A Chapter 8 - Survival / 25
Source URL: https://wwwcdn.imo.org/localresources/en/KnowledgeCentre/IndexofIMOResolutions/MSCResolutions/MSC.385%2894%29.pdf
Local source PDF: MVP/RELEVANT FILES/MSC.385(94).pdf
Local source PDF SHA-256: 6c6a038bff68ee906dd97c41e95fb4442917c014c8e79228f15e80cc5afa5c1f
Locked context path: MVP/SHACL_GENERATION_PIPELINE/experiments/FULL_REPAIR_V2/RUN_01/runs/RUN-IMO-074-20260915T111237373080Z/artifacts/context_pack_initial.json
Original source text:
Whenever the assessment identifies a potential of abandonment onto ice or land, group survival equipment shall be carried, unless an equivalent level of functionality for survival is provided by the ship's normal life-saving appliances.
Normalized requirement:
IF abandonment_onto_ice_or_land_potential = true, THEN group_survival_equipment_carried = true OR normal_life_saving_equivalent_functionality_approval_status = approved.
Required inputs: Operational assessment, group-equipment inventory, and equivalence approval evidence

Locked context, definitions, ownership, units, and dependency contract:
{
  "dependency_contract": {

Original source text:
The ship is discharging sewage that is not comminuted or disinfected in accordance with regulation 11.1.1 of MARPOL Annex IV and at a distance of more than 12 nautical miles from any ice-shelf or fast ice and shall be as far as practicable from areas of ice concentration exceeding 1/10.
Normalized requirement:
IF sewage_discharge_type = not_comminuted_or_disinfected, THEN MARPOL_IV_11_1_1_compliance_status = compliant AND distance_to_nearest_ice_shelf_or_fast_ice_nm > 12; record distance from areas with ice_concentration_tenths > 1.
Required inputs: Discharge type, MARPOL compliance, position, and ice-condition data

Full unmodified selected SHACL:
```turtle
@prefix gen: <urn:nltl:generated-shape:> .
@prefix nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#> .
@prefix qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/> .
@prefix sh: <http://www.w3.org/ns/shacl#> .
@prefix unit: <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/> .
@prefix xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#> .

gen:IMO-116-DirectStaticShape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass nltl:ship ;
    sh:or (
        [
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:sewageDischargeType ;
                sh:datatype xsd:string ;
                sh:hasValue "not_comminuted_or_disinfected"^^xsd:string ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:notComminutedOrDisinfected ;
                sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
                sh:hasValue true ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:marpolAnnexIvRegulation11Point1Point1ComplianceStatus ;
                sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
                sh:hasValue true ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1 ;
                sh:message "MARPOL Annex IV regulation 11.1.1 compliance is required for this discharge type."
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:distanceToNearestIceShelfOrFastIce ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1 ;
                sh:node [
                    sh:class qudt:QuantityValue ;
                    sh:property [
                        sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                        sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
                        sh:minCount 1 ;
                        sh:maxCount 1 ;
                        sh:minExclusive "12"^^xsd:decimal
                    ] ;
                    sh:property [
                        sh:path qudt:unit ;
                        sh:hasValue unit:MI_N ;
                        sh:minCount 1 ;
                        sh:maxCount 1
                    ]
                ] ;
                sh:message "The ship must be more than 12 nautical miles from any ice shelf or fast ice."
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:nearbyIceConcentration ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1 ;
                sh:node [
                    sh:class qudt:QuantityValue ;
                    sh:property [
                        sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                        sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
                        sh:minCount 1 ;
                        sh:maxCount 1 ;
                        sh:minExclusive "0.1"^^xsd:decimal
                    ] ;
                    sh:property [
                        sh:path qudt:unit ;
                        sh:hasValue unit:UNITLESS ;
                        sh:minCount 1 ;
                        sh:maxCount 1
                    ]
                ]
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:distanceFromAreaWithIceConcentrationAboveOneTenth ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1 ;
                sh:node [
                    sh:class qudt:QuantityValue ;
                    sh:property [
                        sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                        sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
                        sh:minCount 1 ;
                        sh:maxCount 1
                    ] ;
                    sh:property [
                        sh:path qudt:unit ;
                        sh:hasValue unit:MI_N ;
                        sh:minCount 1 ;
                        sh:maxCount 1
                    ]
                ] ;
                sh:message "The distance from areas exceeding one tenth ice concentration must be recorded."
            ]
        ]
        [
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:sewageDischargeType ;
                sh:datatype xsd:string ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:notComminutedOrDisinfected ;
                sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
                sh:hasValue false ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ]
        ]
    ) .
```

A. STABLE IDENTITY AND SOURCE EVIDENCE

Clause / section / page: 6.5.4.1 / Bending force, Fex / 45-46
Source URL: https://www.finlex.fi/en/authorities/regulations/traficom-transport-maritime/2021/47238
Local source PDF: MVP/RELEVANT FILES/TRAFICOM.pdf
Local source PDF SHA-256: 8ab845347770c253433b8bad9b1fbe5d6ac71b3d07a1a28dae8a32cfde732ddc
Locked context path: MVP/SHACL_GENERATION_PIPELINE/experiments/FULL_REPAIR_V2/RUN_01/runs/RUN-TRF-101-20260915T135039078068Z/artifacts/context_pack_initial.json
Original source text:
The ultimate load resulting from blade failure by plastic bending around the blade root shall be calculated using F_ex = 300*c*t^2*sigma_ref1/(0.8*D - 2*r) [kN] (6.35), or by an appropriate stress analysis reflecting the non-linear plastic material behaviour of the actual blade. The load is assumed to act at radius 0.8R in the weakest blade direction. A blade is regarded as failed if the tip offset exceeds 10% of propeller diameter D. Use sigma_ref1 = 0.6*sigma_0.2 + 0.4*sigma_u [MPa], with c, t and r representing the actual chord length, maximum thickness, and cylindrical-root radius of the weakest section outside the root fillet.
Normalized requirement:
Determine blade_failure_load_F_ex_kN by an approved non-linear stress analysis OR compute F_ex = 300*c*t^2*sigma_ref1/(0.8*D - 2*r), where sigma_ref1 = 0.6*sigma_0.2 + 0.4*sigma_u. Apply the load at 0.8R in the weakest direction. blade_failure_status = failed IF blade_tip_offset_m > 0.10*propeller_diameter_m.
Required inputs: D_m

```turtle
        <urn:nltl:generated-shape:TRF-101-ApprovedAnalysisBranch>
        <urn:nltl:generated-shape:TRF-101-CalculationBranch>

    sh:sparql [
        sh:message "bladeFailureStatus must be true exactly when blade tip offset exceeds 10 percent of propeller diameter" ;
        sh:select """
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            PREFIX qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/>
            PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>
            SELECT $this
            WHERE {
                $this nltl:bladeTipOffset/qudt:numericValue ?offset ;
                      nltl:propellerDiameter/qudt:numericValue ?diameter ;
                      nltl:bladeFailureStatus ?status .
                FILTER (
                    (?offset > (0.10 * ?diameter) && ?status != true) ||
                    (?offset <= (0.10 * ?diameter) && ?status != false)
                )
            }
        """
    ] .

<urn:nltl:generated-shape:TRF-101-LoadCaseShape>
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:bladeFailureLoadApplicationRadiusRatio ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:node [
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
                sh:minInclusive 0.8 ;
                sh:maxInclusive 0.8 ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:unit ;
                sh:hasValue <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/UNITLESS> ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ]
        ]
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:bladeFailureLoadWeakestDirectionConfirmed ;
        sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:hasValue true
    ] .

<urn:nltl:generated-shape:TRF-101-ApprovedAnalysisBranch>
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:approvedNonlinearBladeStressAnalysis ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1
    ] .

<urn:nltl:generated-shape:TRF-101-CalculationBranch>
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:bladeChordLengthAtWeakestSection ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:bladeCylindricalRootRadius ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:bladeMaximumThicknessAtWeakestSection ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:bladeMinimumYieldStrength ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:bladeReferenceStrength1 ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:bladeUltimateTensileStrength ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1
    ] ;
    sh:sparql [
        sh:message "bladeFailureUltimateLoad and bladeReferenceStrength1 must exactly satisfy the declared direct calculation" ;
        sh:select """
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            PREFIX qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/>
            PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>
            SELECT $this
            WHERE {
                $this nltl:bladeChordLengthAtWeakestSection/qudt:numericValue ?c ;
                      nltl:bladeMaximumThicknessAtWeakestSection/qudt:numericValue ?t ;
                      nltl:bladeCylindricalRootRadius/qudt:numericValue ?r ;
                      nltl:bladeMinimumYieldStrength/qudt:numericValue ?yield ;
                      nltl:bladeUltimateTensileStrength/qudt:numericValue ?ultimate ;
                      nltl:bladeReferenceStrength1/qudt:numericValue ?reference ;
                      nltl:bladeFailureUltimateLoad/qudt:numericValue ?load ;
                      nltl:propellerDiameter/qudt:numericValue ?diameter .
                BIND ((0.6 * ?yield) + (0.4 * ?ultimate) AS ?expectedReference)
                BIND ((300 * ?c * ?t * ?t * ?expectedReference) /
                      ((0.8 * ?diameter) - (2 * ?r)) AS ?expectedLoad)
                FILTER (
                    ?reference != ?expectedReference ||
                    ?load != ?expectedLoad
                )
            }
        """
    ] .
```

```turtle
        sh:message "The calculated blade reference strength or ultimate load does not match the required formula." ;
        sh:select """
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            PREFIX qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/>
            PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>
            SELECT $this
            WHERE {
                $this nltl:bladeChordLengthAtWeakestSection/qudt:numericValue ?c ;
                      nltl:bladeMaximumThicknessAtWeakestSection/qudt:numericValue ?t ;
                      nltl:bladeCylindricalRootRadius/qudt:numericValue ?r ;

                      nltl:bladeFailureUltimateLoad/qudt:numericValue ?load ;
                      nltl:propellerDiameter/qudt:numericValue ?diameter .
                BIND ((0.6 * ?yield) + (0.4 * ?ultimate) AS ?expectedReference)
                BIND (300 * ?c * ?t * ?t * ?expectedReference /
                      (0.8 * ?diameter - 2 * ?r) AS ?expectedLoad)
                BIND ((1e-6 * ABS(?expectedReference)) + 1e-9 AS ?referenceTolerance)
                BIND ((1e-6 * ABS(?expectedLoad)) + 1e-9 AS ?loadTolerance)
                FILTER (
                    (0.8 * ?diameter - 2 * ?r <= 0) ||
                    (?reference - ?expectedReference > ?referenceTolerance) ||
                    (?expectedReference - ?reference > ?referenceTolerance) ||
                    (?load - ?expectedLoad > ?loadTolerance) ||
                    (?expectedLoad - ?load > ?loadTolerance)
                )
            }
        """
    ] ;
    sh:sparql [
        sh:message "Blade failure status must be true exactly when tip offset exceeds 10% of propeller diameter." ;
        sh:select """
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            PREFIX qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/>
            PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>
            SELECT $this
            WHERE {
                $this nltl:bladeTipOffset/qudt:numericValue ?offset ;
                      nltl:propellerDiameter/qudt:numericValue ?diameter ;
                      nltl:bladeFailureStatus ?status .
                FILTER (
                    (?offset > (0.10 * ?diameter) && ?status != true) ||
                    (?offset <= (0.10 * ?diameter) && ?status != false)
                )
            }
        """
    ] .

```

```turtle
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:bladeFailureStatus ;
        sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:hasLoadCase ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:node [
            sh:class nltl:loadCase ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:bladeFailureLoadApplicationRadiusRatio ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1 ;
                sh:node [
                    sh:property [
                        sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                        sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
                        sh:minCount 1 ;
                        sh:maxCount 1
                    ] ;
                    sh:property [
                        sh:path qudt:unit ;
                        sh:minCount 1 ;
                        sh:maxCount 1 ;
                        sh:hasValue <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/UNITLESS>
                    ]
                ]
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:bladeFailureLoadWeakestDirectionConfirmed ;
                sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1 ;
                sh:hasValue true
            ]
        ]
    ] ;
    sh:or (
        [
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:approvedNonlinearBladeStressAnalysis ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:node [
                    sh:class nltl:approvalRecord
                ]
            ]
        ]
        [
            sh:sparql [
                sh:message "The reported blade failure ultimate load and reference strength must satisfy the declared direct calculation within a relative tolerance of 1e-6, with an absolute fallback of 1e-6." ;
                sh:select """
                    PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
                    PREFIX qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/>
                    PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>
                    SELECT $this
                    WHERE {
                        $this nltl:bladeChordLengthAtWeakestSection ?cNode ;
                              nltl:bladeCylindricalRootRadius ?rNode ;
                              nltl:bladeMaximumThicknessAtWeakestSection ?tNode ;
                              nltl:bladeMinimumYieldStrength ?yieldNode ;
                              nltl:bladeUltimateTensileStrength ?ultimateNode ;
                              nltl:bladeReferenceStrength1 ?referenceNode ;
                              nltl:bladeFailureUltimateLoad ?loadNode ;
                              nltl:propellerDiameter ?diameterNode ;
                              nltl:hasLoadCase ?case .

                        ?cNode qudt:numericValue ?c .
                        ?rNode qudt:numericValue ?r .
                        ?tNode qudt:numericValue ?t .
                        ?yieldNode qudt:numericValue ?yield .
                        ?ultimateNode qudt:numericValue ?ultimate .
                        ?referenceNode qudt:numericValue ?reference .
                        ?loadNode qudt:numericValue ?load .
                        ?diameterNode qudt:numericValue ?diameter .

                        ?case nltl:bladeFailureLoadApplicationRadiusRatio ?ratioNode .
                        ?ratioNode qudt:numericValue ?ratio .

                        BIND((0.6 * ?yield) + (0.4 * ?ultimate) AS ?expectedReference)
                        BIND((300 * ?c * ?t * ?t * ?expectedReference) /
                             ((0.8 * ?diameter) - (2 * ?r)) AS ?expectedLoad)
                        BIND(IF(ABS(?expectedReference) * 0.000001 > 0.000001,
                                ABS(?expectedReference) * 0.000001,
                                0.000001) AS ?referenceTolerance)
                        BIND(IF(ABS(?expectedLoad) * 0.000001 > 0.000001,
                                ABS(?expectedLoad) * 0.000001,
                                0.000001) AS ?loadTolerance)

                        FILTER(
                            (?reference < (?expectedReference - ?referenceTolerance)) ||
                            (?reference > (?expectedReference + ?referenceTolerance)) ||
                            (?load < (?expectedLoad - ?loadTolerance)) ||
                            (?load > (?expectedLoad + ?loadTolerance))
                        )
                    }
                """
            ]
        ]
    ) ;
    sh:sparql [
        sh:message "Blade failure status must be true when tip offset exceeds 10% of propeller diameter and false otherwise." ;
        sh:select """
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            PREFIX qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/>
            PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>
            SELECT $this
            WHERE {
                $this nltl:bladeTipOffset ?offsetNode ;
                      nltl:propellerDiameter ?diameterNode ;
                      nltl:bladeFailureStatus ?status .

                ?offsetNode qudt:numericValue ?offset .
                ?diameterNode qudt:numericValue ?diameter .

                FILTER(
                    (?offset > (0.10 * ?diameter) && ?status != true) ||
                    (?offset <= (0.10 * ?diameter) && ?status != false)
                )
            }
        """
    ] .
```

Full unmodified selected SHACL:
```turtle
@prefix nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#> .
@prefix qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/> .
@prefix sh: <http://www.w3.org/ns/shacl#> .
@prefix unit: <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/> .
@prefix xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#> .

<urn:nltl:generated-shape:IMO-008> a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass nltl:ship ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:iceTypeClassification ;
        sh:nodeKind sh:IRI ;
        sh:node [
            sh:class nltl:iceTypeValue
        ] ;
        sh:message "A ship may have an ice type classification." 
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:iceThickness ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:node [
            sh:class qudt:QuantityValue ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:unit ;
                sh:hasValue unit:M ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ]
        ] ;
        sh:message "Any recorded ice thickness must be a single metre-valued numeric quantity."
    ] ;
    sh:sparql [
        sh:message "For thin first-year ice, ice thickness must be between 0.3 m and 0.7 m inclusive." ;
        sh:select """
PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
PREFIX qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/>
PREFIX unit: <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/>

SELECT $this
WHERE {
    $this nltl:iceTypeClassification nltl:thinFirstYearIce .
    FILTER NOT EXISTS {
        $this nltl:iceThickness ?thickness .
        ?thickness a qudt:QuantityValue ;
                   qudt:numericValue ?value ;
                   qudt:unit unit:M .
        FILTER (
            isNumeric(?value) &&
            ?value >= 0.3 &&
            ?value <= 0.7
        )
    }
}
```

Full unmodified selected SHACL:
```turtle
@prefix gen: <urn:nltl:generated-shape:> .
@prefix nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#> .
@prefix qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/> .
@prefix sh: <http://www.w3.org/ns/shacl#> .
@prefix unit: <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/> .
@prefix xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#> .

gen:IMO_008_ThinFirstYearIceShape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass nltl:ship ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:iceTypeClassification ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:message "A ship must provide an ice type classification to determine applicability of the thin first-year ice thickness range."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:iceThickness ;
        sh:node [
            sh:class qudt:QuantityValue ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:unit ;
                sh:hasValue unit:M ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ]
        ]
    ] ;
    sh:sparql [
        a sh:SPARQLConstraint ;
        sh:message "For thin first-year ice, ice thickness must be between 0.3 m and 0.7 m inclusive." ;
        sh:select """
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            PREFIX qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/>
            PREFIX unit: <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/>
            PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>

            SELECT $this
            WHERE {
                $this nltl:iceTypeClassification nltl:thinFirstYearIce .
                FILTER NOT EXISTS {
                    $this nltl:iceThickness ?quantity .
                    ?quantity qudt:numericValue ?value ;
                              qudt:unit unit:M .
                    FILTER (
                        datatype(?value) = xsd:decimal &&
                        ?value >= "0.3"^^xsd:decimal &&
                        ?value <= "0.7"^^xsd:decimal
                    )
                }
            }
        """
    ] .
```

```turtle
@prefix nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#> .
@prefix qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/> .
@prefix sh: <http://www.w3.org/ns/shacl#> .
@prefix unit: <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/> .
@prefix xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#> .

<urn:nltl:generated-shape:TRF-086> a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass nltl:ship ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:propellerType ;
        sh:datatype xsd:string ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:message "A propeller type is required to determine applicability of the CP propeller rule."
    ] ;
    sh:sparql [
        sh:message "For a CP propeller, pitchValueKnown must be recorded as a boolean." ;
        sh:select """
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>
            SELECT $this
            WHERE {
                $this nltl:propellerType ?type .
                FILTER (?type = "CP"^^xsd:string)
                FILTER NOT EXISTS {
                    $this nltl:pitchValueKnown ?known .
                    FILTER (datatype(?known) = xsd:boolean)
                }
            }
        """
    ] ;
    sh:sparql [
        sh:message "For a CP propeller with known pitch, propellerPitchAtMcrBollard must be supplied as a metre quantity." ;
        sh:select """
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            PREFIX qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/>
            PREFIX unit: <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/>
            PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>
            SELECT $this
            WHERE {
                $this nltl:propellerType ?type ;
                      nltl:pitchValueKnown ?known .
                FILTER (?type = "CP"^^xsd:string && ?known = true)
                FILTER NOT EXISTS {
                    $this nltl:propellerPitchAtMcrBollard ?bollard .
                    ?bollard qudt:numericValue ?value ;
                             qudt:unit unit:M .
                    FILTER (datatype(?value) = xsd:decimal)
                }
            }
        """
    ] ;
    sh:sparql [
        sh:message "For a CP propeller with unknown pitch, both pitch quantities must be supplied in metres." ;
        sh:select """
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            PREFIX qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/>
            PREFIX unit: <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/>
            PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>
            SELECT $this
            WHERE {
                $this nltl:propellerType ?type ;
                      nltl:pitchValueKnown ?known .
                FILTER (?type = "CP"^^xsd:string && ?known = false)
                FILTER NOT EXISTS {
                    $this nltl:propellerPitchAtMcrBollard ?bollard ;
                          nltl:propellerPitchAtMcrFreeRunning ?free .
                    ?bollard qudt:numericValue ?bollardValue ;
                             qudt:unit unit:M .
                    ?free qudt:numericValue ?freeValue ;
                          qudt:unit unit:M .
                    FILTER (
                        datatype(?bollardValue) = xsd:decimal &&
                        datatype(?freeValue) = xsd:decimal
                    )
                }
            }
        """
    ] ;
    sh:sparql [
        sh:message "For a CP propeller with unknown pitch, propellerPitchAtMcrBollard must equal 0.7 times propellerPitchAtMcrFreeRunning exactly." ;
        sh:select """
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            PREFIX qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/>
            PREFIX unit: <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/>
            PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>
            SELECT $this
            WHERE {
                $this nltl:propellerType ?type ;
                      nltl:pitchValueKnown ?known ;
                      nltl:propellerPitchAtMcrBollard ?bollard ;
                      nltl:propellerPitchAtMcrFreeRunning ?free .
                FILTER (?type = "CP"^^xsd:string && ?known = false)
                ?bollard qudt:numericValue ?bollardValue ;
                         qudt:unit unit:M .
                ?free qudt:numericValue ?freeValue ;
                      qudt:unit unit:M .
                FILTER (
                    datatype(?bollardValue) = xsd:decimal &&
                    datatype(?freeValue) = xsd:decimal
                )
                BIND ("0.7"^^xsd:decimal * ?freeValue AS ?expected)
                FILTER (?bollardValue != ?expected)
            }
        """
    ] .
```

```turtle
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:propellerType ;

                sh:hasValue "CP"^^xsd:string ;

                sh:maxCount 1
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:pitchValueKnown ;
                sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
                sh:hasValue false ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:propellerPitchAtMcrFreeRunning ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1 ;
                sh:node [
                    sh:class qudt:QuantityValue ;
                    sh:property [
                        sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                        sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
                        sh:minCount 1 ;
                        sh:maxCount 1
                    ] ;
                    sh:property [
                        sh:path qudt:unit ;
                        sh:hasValue unit:M ;
                        sh:minCount 1 ;
                        sh:maxCount 1
                    ]
                ]
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:propellerPitchAtMcrBollard ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1 ;
                sh:node [
                    sh:class qudt:QuantityValue ;
                    sh:property [
                        sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                        sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
                        sh:minCount 1 ;
                        sh:maxCount 1
                    ] ;
                    sh:property [
                        sh:path qudt:unit ;
                        sh:hasValue unit:M ;
                        sh:minCount 1 ;
                        sh:maxCount 1
                    ]
                ]
            ] ;
            sh:sparql [
                sh:message "For a CP propeller with unknown pitch value, propeller pitch at MCR in bollard condition must equal 0.7 times propeller pitch at MCR in free-running condition within numerical tolerance." ;
                sh:select """
                    PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
                    PREFIX qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/>
                    PREFIX unit: <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/>

                    PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>

                    SELECT $this
                    WHERE {
                        $this nltl:propellerPitchAtMcrBollard ?bollardQuantity .
                        ?bollardQuantity qudt:numericValue ?bollardValue ;
                                          qudt:unit unit:M .
                        $this nltl:propellerPitchAtMcrFreeRunning ?freeRunningQuantity .
                        ?freeRunningQuantity qudt:numericValue ?freeRunningValue ;
                                             qudt:unit unit:M .
                        FILTER (datatype(?bollardValue) = xsd:decimal)
                        FILTER (datatype(?freeRunningValue) = xsd:decimal)
                        BIND (0.7 * ?freeRunningValue AS ?expectedValue)
                        FILTER (
                            ABS(?bollardValue - ?expectedValue) >
                            (ABS(?expectedValue) * 0.000001 + 0.000000001)
                        )
                    }
                """
            ]
        ]
        [
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:propellerType ;
                sh:datatype xsd:string ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ] ;
            sh:sparql [
                sh:message "This alternative applies only when the propeller type is not CP." ;
                sh:select """
                    PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
                    PREFIX sh: <http://www.w3.org/ns/shacl#>

                    SELECT $this
                    WHERE {
```

Q2 Ownership / paths: Are target ownership and property paths correct?
Q3 Required constraints: Are all mandatory obligations represented?
Q4 Units / calculation: Are quantities, units, formulae, and comparisons correct?
Q5 Branches / boundaries: Are alternatives, exceptions, equality boundaries, and conditionals correct?
Q6 No extra restrictions: Does the shape avoid requirements not supported by the source?

REQUIREMENT 090: IMO-068

A. STABLE IDENTITY AND SOURCE EVIDENCE
Audit order: 90
Batch: BATCH_09
Requirement ID: IMO-068
Source family: IMO
Verification mode: DIRECT_STATIC
Source document: International Code for Ships Operating in Polar Waters (Polar Code)
Source revision: Resolution MSC.385(94), adopted 21 Nov 2014; consolidated with MEPC.264(68), adopted 15 May 2015
Clause / section / page: 8.3.2.1 / Part I-A Chapter 8 - Evacuation / 24
Source URL: https://wwwcdn.imo.org/localresources/en/KnowledgeCentre/IndexofIMOResolutions/MSCResolutions/MSC.385%2894%29.pdf
Local source PDF: MVP/RELEVANT FILES/MSC.385(94).pdf

Locked context path: MVP/SHACL_GENERATION_PIPELINE/experiments/FULL_REPAIR_V2/RUN_01/runs/RUN-IMO-068-20260915T110754053541Z/artifacts/context_pack_initial.json
Original source text:
Ships shall have means to ensure safe evacuation of persons, including safe deployment of survival equipment, when operating in ice-covered waters, or directly onto the ice, as applicable.
Normalized requirement:
IF ship_operates_in_ice_covered_waters = true OR direct_evacuation_onto_ice_applicable = true, THEN safe_evacuation_means_present = true AND survival_equipment_deployment_approval_status = approved.
Required inputs: Operating condition, evacuation arrangement, and deployment evidence

```turtle
@prefix nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#> .
@prefix sh: <http://www.w3.org/ns/shacl#> .
@prefix xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#> .
@prefix gen: <urn:nltl:generated-shape:> .

gen:IMO_068_ShipShape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass nltl:ship ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:shipOperatesInIceCoveredWaters ;
        sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:message "The ship's ice-covered-water operating condition must be recorded as a single boolean value."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:directEvacuationOntoIceApplicable ;
        sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:message "Whether direct evacuation onto ice is applicable must be recorded as a single boolean value."
    ] ;
    sh:or (
        gen:IMO_068_IceCoveredWatersBranch
        gen:IMO_068_DirectOntoIceBranch
        gen:IMO_068_NeitherApplicableBranch
    ) .

gen:IMO_068_IceCoveredWatersBranch
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:shipOperatesInIceCoveredWaters ;
        sh:hasValue true
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:safeEvacuationMeansPresent ;
        sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:hasValue true ;
        sh:message "Safe evacuation means must be present when operating in ice-covered waters."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:survivalEquipmentDeploymentApprovalStatus ;
        sh:class nltl:evidenceState ;
        sh:nodeKind sh:IRI ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:hasValue nltl:evidenceStateApproved ;
        sh:message "Survival equipment deployment must have approved status when operating in ice-covered waters."
    ] .

gen:IMO_068_DirectOntoIceBranch
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:directEvacuationOntoIceApplicable ;
        sh:hasValue true
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:safeEvacuationMeansPresent ;
        sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:hasValue true ;
        sh:message "Safe evacuation means must be present when direct evacuation onto ice is applicable."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:survivalEquipmentDeploymentApprovalStatus ;
        sh:class nltl:evidenceState ;
        sh:nodeKind sh:IRI ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:hasValue nltl:evidenceStateApproved ;
        sh:message "Survival equipment deployment must have approved status when direct evacuation onto ice is applicable."
    ] .

gen:IMO_068_NeitherApplicableBranch
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:shipOperatesInIceCoveredWaters ;
        sh:hasValue false
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:directEvacuationOntoIceApplicable ;
        sh:hasValue false
    ] .
```
